## Meta hackathon — plan v2 "BitPOS × Muse" and Stripe agent-payment probe — 27 October 2026

- Meta hackathon work happens only on `hackathon/meta-2026` in the worktree `~/Desktop/BitPOS-Meta-2026`; the pre-hackathon baseline is tag `meta-hackathon-baseline` (`9eb34ea`, pushed 24 October). Plan, demo script, provenance and evidence live in `meta-hackathon/`.
- Owner decisions: Startup track; cloud-only Meta inference; promotions need human approval first, with an owner "Allow All" policy for later in-bounds auto-approval; NTAG424 DNA sticker tap + Messenger consent for identity (no PN532, no firmware change, no face recognition, no social tracking); Stripe test mode + Muse-style agent payment instead of crypto in the demo; the agent-payment demo uses a US-country Stripe sandbox, labelled as such. The earlier "BitPOS Sight" camera plan is dropped.
- Built: Meta model router, offer leash (to become `offer-policy.ts`), promotion-pipeline bridge, migration `300_meta_ai.sql`, Stripe sandbox module `apps/api/src/meta/stripe.ts` (Shared Payment Token charge, webhook signature verification), probe `tools/meta/stripe-smoke.ts`. Validation: meta tests 20/20, repo `tsc` pass, migrations `001`–`137` + `300` on disposable PostgreSQL 17 (029 needed its history guard removed in a temporary copy only).
- Live sandbox check: Stripe sandbox "BitPOS Meta US demo" (`acct_1UVDEKJ3OQ9SsvzG`, country US, test only) charged an agent token through `stripe.ts`; a retry did not double-charge; reused, over-limit and declined tokens failed. The key lives only in `~/.bitpos-meta/stripe-test.env`. Evidence: `meta-hackathon/evidence/2026-10-27-*.md`.
- 28 October: NTAG424 SUN verification (NXP vector passes), migration 301 (replay-safe tap sessions), migration 302 + `/meta/` routes (tap, Messenger webhook, consent, sign-in link, customer session) mounted in `apps/api/src/index.ts`. Validation: meta tests 31/31 including an end-to-end flow on disposable PostgreSQL under RLS; `tsc` pass. Evidence: `meta-hackathon/evidence/2026-10-28-tap-messenger-session.md`.
- 28 October (later): offer on tap — Muse Spark picks one approved, server-priced offer per tap (id enum, digit-free reasons, deterministic fallback), stored once per tap with the model invocation; shown in Messenger and on the web. Migration 303. Meta tests 36/36 with the database; `tsc` pass. Evidence: `meta-hackathon/evidence/2026-10-28-offer-on-tap.md`. Meta Model API: `https://api.meta.ai/v1`, `muse-spark-1.3`.
- Live Muse Spark works: `muse-spark-1.3` via `https://api.meta.ai/v1/chat/completions` picked offers in 4/4 runs (3.2–3.5 s) after setting `reasoning_effort: minimal` and a 2,000-token budget (the first call spent its whole 300-token budget on reasoning). Key only in `~/.bitpos-meta/meta-api.env`.
- Stripe checkout on the tap page: own ledger `meta_tap_orders` (migration 304), re-quoted price, PAID only from a signed webhook or a server-side Checkout lookup, stock decremented once. Live: THB Checkout Session created and looked up in the US sandbox; paying on the hosted page not yet done. Meta tests 39/39 with the database. Evidence: `meta-hackathon/evidence/2026-10-28-tap-checkout.md`.
- Real Stripe webhook works end to end: Cloudflare quick tunnel (this Mac only) → `tools/meta/meta-server.ts` (/meta only, disposable DB) → sandbox `payment_intent.succeeded` marked the order PAID and took stock once; Stripe shows the delivery acknowledged. Evidence: `meta-hackathon/evidence/2026-10-28-tunnel-webhooks.md`.
- Messenger live: Page "Bitpos Muse Demo" and a development-mode Meta app; webhook set via the Graph API. A real tap → referral → consent → sign-in link worked on the quick tunnel (12:16–12:17 ICT).

Credit guard of 10 model calls per customer per hour with a free fallback matcher. Migration 307 applied on OCI after a backup. Meta tests 53/53. Muse calls on OCI so far: 2. Offers go through the inherited `productOffer()`. Migration 308 applied on OCI after a backup (`bitpos-20261028T120133Z.dump`). Meta tests 57/57 (twice). - 29 October (owner request): reserved the other streams' hostnames on the BitPOS tunnel. `sol-pay` → 127.0.0.1:4323 and `sol-pos` → :4324 are aliases of the current Solana apps (same responses as `pay.`/`pos.`; the Solana apps' own origin settings still name pay./pos.). `paypal-pay` and `paypal-pos` → :3103, a port reserved for a future PayPal container (502 until one listens there). The PayPal and Solana streams own what runs behind their names.

- 29 October (later): closed three design gaps. MP19 `/meta/staff/payouts` (owner/manager) reads the Stripe test balance and the last 10 payouts through `listPayouts()` in `stripe.ts`; it is read only and refuses live-mode answers. MP21 `/meta/staff/users` (owner/manager) lists the members with their roles, shows no emails, and shows the role-permission table taken from the actual checks. Membership changes still go through the admin script. CD01–CD03: at ≥1000px meta-pay shows the menu in two columns and the cart as lines plus a sticky summary. Meta tests 72/72 against the local DB. Deployed on OCI and checked live (real sandbox balance shown, 3 members listed).
- 29 October (later): first Muse Café offer. Muse Spark drafted Latte −10% for 8 h (1 model call) and the owner approved it once, with no Allow All; it now shows on meta-pay. Messenger UI aligned with design C08–C15: quick-reply chips (Menu · My orders · Book) under the last reply (not under booking questions); menu cards have "Add" and "Details" (meta-pay item page, shop merchant only). The chat cart keeps no state: the cart rides in the postback payload (≤10 lines, strict regex), and the server re-prices it on every step. Pay makes one cart order with channel `chat` and link_id required (migration 315, applied on OCI after backup `bitpos-20261029T034726Z-pre315.dump`, 298 data sections). The paid webhook sends a Messenger receipt with the shop name, subtotal and the approved-offer adjustment. My orders is now cards: bookings with Cancel, cart orders with Pay while pending, chat orders. Meta tests 73/73. Not yet checked in the real Messenger app.
- 29 October (later): voice order page `/meta/order` rebuilt from design C11. It has a big mic button that pulses while listening, a live transcript, a Thai/English switch and a typed fallback, plus suggestion chips taken from the menu. Answers show as bubbles, with a photo quote card or tappable menu rows; tapping a menu row goes to `/meta/order/add`, which quotes with no model call. Before, a speech error left the button silent; now every speech error says why: mic permission/macOS privacy, Safari Dictation off, Chrome speech network, no speech, no mic. `Permissions-Policy: microphone=(self)` added, and the CSP now allows menu photos. Messenger welcome screen: `BITPOS_GET_STARTED` had no handler (the Get Started button got no reply) and is now handled, as is `BITPOS_HELP`. A connected customer gets a 3-card picture menu (order/voice, book, my orders). A stranger gets how-to-connect, web menu and privacy cards; a stranger never reaches the agent. Messenger profile updated on the Page (200): greeting with first name, 4 conversation starters, persistent menu Home/Menu/My orders/Book/Voice/Forget me. Meta tests 74/74. Not yet checked on a real phone or MacBook mic.
- 29 October (later): the paid Messenger receipt is now a picture (shop, pickup code, lines with menu photos, discount, total). It is drawn as SVG and rasterised by resvg with the repo's Sarabun font, served at `/meta/receipt/:id.png?k=HMAC`; paid chat orders only, no customer identity on it. A short text with chips follows. If the picture is refused or resvg is missing, the old receipt template is sent instead. The Meta container now runs `bitpos-meta-base:20261029-r1` (infra/meta/Dockerfile = pinned base + @resvg/resvg-js 2.6.2, linked in, base untouched); resvg is not added to the root package.json because pnpm would re-resolve the `latest` web deps. Checked live: a real paid order rendered (200 PNG), a forged key gives 404. meta-pos bookings now has a floor plan like the POS shop floor (window, coffee bar, entrance), with each table's live state from `floorPlan()`: free / booked / check-in (pulsing) / seated, plus pickup code, party, times, more bookings today, open table orders and Minis. Tapping a table jumps to its booking row. The voice order page was restyled as a modern assistant: dark thread, a gradient orb that follows the mic level (Web Audio, local only), a glass dock with input and chips. Meta tests 76/76.
- 29 October (later): voice ordering now uses Muse Voice Transcribe (`muse-voice-transcribe-1.0`, `POST /v1/asr/transcribe`, per dev.meta.ai docs). Before, on the owner's MacBook, Chrome's own speech service failed with a `network` error. Now the page records a push-to-talk clip itself as 16 kHz mono PCM WAV: the orb follows the level, it stops after 1.2 s of quiet or 15 s, and the clip is sent to `/meta/order/voice` (signed-in customer, same origin, WAV header checked, ≤45 s, shares the 10 model-calls/hour/customer cap). The server forwards it once with the menu names as `keywords` and Thai/English `languageBias`, and returns only the transcript, which goes through the same agent as typed text. Audio is never stored. Browser speech recognition is now only the fallback. One live call (1 credit) with a macOS-synthesised Thai clip came back as text in 3.8 s. Meta tests 77/77. Not yet checked with a real voice on the MacBook.

For a Messenger test without NFC, `tools/meta/sim-tap.ts` issued a one-time tap link for sticker muse-t1.
  - **Language:** a `bitpos_lang` cookie set from the EN/ไทย toggle via `/meta/staff/lang`; the way back must be a /meta/staff path. `staff-i18n.ts` maps each Thai run in text nodes and placeholder/title/aria-label through one table (277 phrases). Older "ไทย / English" strings keep the side for the page language. Menu and product names use the product's own English name (`nm`), and word-order-sensitive sentences use `L(th,en)`. A test renders every staff page plus a counter bill in English and fails on any Thai left (except the ไทย toggle); it also checks each page in Thai.
  - **Layout:** under 860 px the sidebar becomes a sticky top bar with shop, language and a ☰ menu, a full-screen icon grid with role and sign-out. It is a checkbox toggle; a `<details>` element was invisible because Chrome clips its content. Tables scroll inside `.tw`, grids collapse to 1–2 columns, and the floor plan shows 2 tables per row. Fixed a `.top` class clash on floor tiles. Checked live on 375 px and desktop, EN and TH. Meta tests 80/80.
  1. **Several items in one sentence:** a new agent step `cart` (≤5 lines from the menu enum, merged). The free matcher also finds every menu name (longest first), with a digit or Thai number word after it, or a digit before it. Chat replies with the cart card; the voice page shows a cart card, and `/meta/order/cartpay` makes one cart order (`web`, link_id) at the server price. Real Muse Spark: "ขอลาเต้สองแก้วกับครวซองหนึ่งชิ้นค่ะ" → Latte×2 + Croissant×1 in 2.2 s.
  2. **Photo order:** `planPhotoOrder`/`matchPhoto`. Muse Spark sees the photo and picks a listed item (or none), plus ≤2 alternatives and a confidence; medium or low confidence is said to the customer. On the voice page (📷) the page shrinks the photo to a 768 px JPEG, the server checks the JPEG magic and size ≤650 KB, and the result is tappable rows (no model call). In Messenger an image attachment works too, but only from fbcdn.net/fbsbx.com https URLs. Nothing is stored and there is no guess without a model. On OCI `META_AI_META_API_IMAGES=1` was missing (photo → NO_PROVIDER_AVAILABLE); it was added after an env backup. Real call: the matcha photo → Matcha Latte, high, 5.8 s.
  3. **Live transcript:** a WebSocket proxy `/meta/order/live` (`live-voice.ts`, ws@8.18.3, already a root dependency), hooked in meta-server's `upgrade`. It checks same origin, the customer session and the shared model-call cap. It sends the handshake (key server-side, PCM_24KHZ, PUSH_TO_TALK, Thai/English bias, menu keywords), relays 24 kHz PCM16, and passes back only {transcript, final}; 20 s cap. The page streams while it records and falls back to the WAV file endpoint if the live session gives nothing. Real session: partials every 0.2–0.5 s, closed 1000. Without the language bias the first words came out as Vietnamese; with it, the Thai was correct. Meta tests 80/80. Not yet checked with a real voice in the browser.
- 30 October (later): Muse tool calling (owner picked 1–3).
  - **`router.invokeTools`:** the Meta Model API chat-completions loop with tools and parallel_tool_calls. Arguments are Zod-checked and run on the server, results go back, at most 4 rounds, in-order call list, and every round is recorded in meta_ai_invocations (tasks agent_tools and owner_insights).
  - **Cart conversation (`cart-agent.ts`, migration 320 `meta_chat_carts`):** Messenger free text and the voice page edit one stored cart with 8 tools (get_menu, view_cart, add_item, set_quantity, swap_item, clear_cart, checkout, booking). Replies lose any number the model writes; the priced cart card follows. Booking and no-model cases use the old one-step path. Cart buttons also save the stored cart.
  - **Owner assistant (`owner-assistant.ts`, `/meta/staff/assistant`, Muse tab):** Muse Wonder (`muse-wonder-1.0`, fallback to Spark) with 6 read-only tenant-bound tools (sales by day/channel, top items, orders by hour, stock, offers and bookings). The page shows each tool's data under the answer. Live: muse-wonder-1.0 called sales_by_day and answered with the matching numbers.
  - **Facebook Page post drafts (`social.ts`, migration 321):** Muse Image square post from the real menu photo, plus Muse Spark words (number-free); the server adds price, discount and end time and the AI-edited note. An owner or manager edits the caption and presses Post (Graph /{page}/photos); errors are kept and Post can be tried again. Live: draft made. Not posted — publishing is the owner's action, and the Page token may lack pages_manage_posts.
  - **Fix:** English staff pages no longer translate text inside textarea/pre (they had rewritten the caption).
  - **OCI:** migrations 320–321 applied after backup `bitpos-20261029T182844Z-pre320.dump`. Meta tests 92/92.
- 30 October (later): offer animations and two-item offers (owner request).
  - **Animations (`anim.ts`, `gif.ts`):** a looping GIF made on the server from the offer picture. Frames are SVG rendered by resvg, with a slow zoom and pan, the discount badge popping in and pulsing, a light sweep, twinkling sparkles and the names fading in. Our own GIF encoder: fixed 8×8×4 palette, ordered dither, LZW. Sizes are 480 px (Messenger, meta-pay) and 240 px (BitPOS Mini). Each is made once and cached in `meta_offer_animations`. URLs carry a tag (`…/480-<tag>.gif`) so Cloudflare, which keeps GIFs for 4 h, never serves an old one.
  - **WebP:** Muse Image answers WebP, which resvg cannot read, so the Meta image is now `bitpos-meta-base:20261029-r2` (r1 + sharp 0.33.5, linked in) and sharp turns WebP into PNG first.
  - **Memory:** frames are kept as palette indices. The container limit went from 256 MB to 512 MB, because a 480 px build peaked at 386 MB on top of the ~200 MB the server idles at; at 256 MB it was killed.
  - **Two-item offers (`combos.ts`, migration 322):** Muse Spark picks two items from the list, a 5–20% discount on the pair, and how long it runs, with digit-free reasons; the template fallback is a drink plus a bakery item at 10% for 6 h. An owner or manager approves, rejects or stops it on the Promotions page.
    - On approval, Muse Image `/v1/images/generations` draws a new picture of the pair, labelled as an AI picture. If it fails, the first item's real photo is used.
    - The cart prices it on the server: per pair of A and B, X% off both unit prices, and each unit counts in one pair only. Checkout charges that one total.
  - **Where customers see it:**
    - Messenger: after a tap, the chosen offer's animation, then the offer card, then the live pair's animation and an "Add both" button.
    - meta-pay: the menu shows the pair card with its animation and "Add both"; an item on offer shows its animation; the cart shows a "Pair deal" line.
    - BitPOS Mini: the idle screen and the welcome-after-tap screen get `anim`, which is the tapped customer's offer, else the pair, else a live offer.
  - **Firmware (`bitpos_meta.c`):** downloads the same-origin path into PSRAM (at most 900 KB, must start with `GIF8`, retried every 30 s), plays it with `lv_gif` from memory at 240×240 with the text on the right, and frees the old buffer after the swap. It builds (1.44 MB, 31% of the slot free). Flashed on 30 October into app1 of the owner's Mini (MAC checked; the previous app1 was saved privately in `~/.bitpos-meta/firmware-backups/`, mode 600; write hash verified). After boot the Mini downloaded `/meta/anim/combo/…/240-<tag>.gif` once and kept polling `/meta/device/state` every ~6 s with no reboot. The owner saw a black box: the first version handed lv_gif a variable source with a rounded clip. It now follows the Sol terminal's proven `bitpos_media.c` path: the GIF is opened by path through a read-only in-memory lv_fs drive `A:`, has no rounded clip, and is dropped if not loaded. The device log (`BitPOS` tag) shows `META_ANIM loaded 240x240 … mid=6d4900`, which matches LVGL gifdec decoding the same file on the Mac.
  - **Live on OCI:** migration 322 applied after backup `bitpos-20261029T185016Z-pre322.dump`. Muse Spark paired Latte + Croissant −10% for 4 h, the owner approved it, and Muse Image drew the picture in 10 s (WebP). The animation is served at 480 px (1.35 MB, ~4 s the first time) and 240 px (326 KB). The meta-pay cart showed −$0.90 for one pair.
  - **Tests:** `tests/meta-combos.test.ts` checks that the GIF round-trips through a decoder past the 4096-code reset, pair pricing, RLS, the cache, and the device path filter. Meta tests 95/95.
  - **Open:**
    - The owner should confirm by eye that the animation plays smoothly on the Mini's screen.
    - Messenger animation on a real tap.
- Next: NTAG424 sticker provisioning tool; Muse Connector (MCP); Allow All UI; demo video.
- Superseded (kept for history): Messenger (needs the owner signed in to Facebook to create a Page and Meta app), paying a hosted Checkout by hand, a real Messenger app/page and webhook delivery, NTAG424 sticker configuration, Muse connector review, routing `/meta/*` to the API in the tunnel. Next: live Muse Spark call, sticker provisioning tool, Stripe checkout on the tap page.

## PayPal competition strategy revision — 12 ตุลาคม 2026

- ผู้ใช้ขอปรับแผนให้โอกาสชนะ 80%. ชี้แจงว่าไม่มีข้อมูลคู่แข่ง/ผลตัดสินรองรับ probability นี้; ช่วงประมาณการในการสนทนาก่อนหน้าเป็น subjective judgment ไม่ใช่ calibrated model. ไม่รับรอง 80% และไม่แทน win probability ด้วย internal score/usability rate.
- เพิ่ม `PayPal/WIN_STRATEGY_TH.md`; sync README/PLAN/English submission/provenance/sources. เจาะโจทย์ time-limited surplus/stock campaign: merchant intent → bounded real-AI tools/draft → explicit merchant approval + target → PayPal Sandbox verified capture → Mini/browser → evidence trace/outcome. Quantity/time/discount envelope ต้องใช้ stock/reservation transaction เดิม; ไม่ autonomous charging/publish/broadcast.
- ตรวจ official Square AI และ Too Good To Go search extracts เพื่อไม่อ้าง AI merchant/surplus เป็นแนวคิดใหม่; บันทึกว่าไม่ใช่ exhaustive feature audit. วาง merchant discovery, manual/rules/AI comparison, small formative user tests, important-case reliability และ go/no-go gates. ไม่มีสัมภาษณ์/ส่งข้อความ/เก็บ user data จริงในงานนี้. Internal readiness target ≥85/100 ด้านละ ≥15/20 + mandatory gates; ไม่แปลงเป็นเปอร์เซ็นต์ชนะ.
- ตรวจ active OMP owner `post-submit-20261012` และ checkpoint ปัจจุบัน A code review pass/B implementation. เปลี่ยนเฉพาะแผนและ checkpoint ไม่แก้ runtime, schema, source ownership, credentials หรือ deploy/flash. งานใหม่ทั้งหมดยัง planned; ไม่อ้าง AI/PayPal/physical acceptance หรือ business uplift สำเร็จ.
- Validation: เอกสาร PayPal 7 ไฟล์, local Markdown links/fences/newlines และ deadline timezone assertion ผ่าน; `git diff --check` ผ่าน. Next: discovery/access/spike ภายใน 14 ต.ค., payment core gate 20 ต.ค., bounded AI→PayPal flow 25 ต.ค., user evidence/review 3 พ.ย. และเป้าส่ง 11 พ.ย. 18:00 ไทยตามแผน. Scope ยังเป็นการปรับแผน ไม่ได้เริ่ม implementation ใหม่.
